# nexon_x64.dll - unified dossier

**GMS v270**: 4,526,080 bytes, linked 2017-10-27, 265 imported / 6106 exported.
**MSEA**: not shipped in the audited MSEA corpus.
**Status**: GMS-only.

## MSEA build

Not shipped in the audited MSEA corpus - GMS-only module.

## GMS v270 dossier

### Role and load path

This is a native 64-bit Nexon client/platform SDK, not a language runtime or managed-code host. Its 6,106 exports are explained by a large decorated C++ ABI surface: 5,820 exports belong to `log4cxx`, and the remaining surface includes C++ standard-library templates, libssh2, and Nexon's own interface factories. The class names and strings identify account/API, user, friends, callback, patcher, and authentication components. The PE timestamp is 2017-10-27.

The inventory shows no static import of this DLL by another inventoried binary. `MapleStory.exe` statically imports only `nxapi_shutdown` from `nexon_api_x64.dll`; the shim then loads this DLL dynamically with `LoadLibraryW` and resolves `GetDLLInterface`. The shim's normal path-building code combines an NXL-derived directory with `bin\nexon_x64.dll`; that is static code evidence, not an observed runtime load. See [nexon_api_x64.md](nexon_api_x64.md) for the full loader chain.

### Image and export structure

- AMD64 PE, image base `0x180000000`, size 4,526,080 bytes (about 4.32 MiB), timestamp 2017-10-27.
- Exports: 6,106; no forwarders listed in the supplied export table.
- Purpose buckets below partition the exports by name/content. Prefix buckets are a separate, orthogonal view of the same 6,106 names.

#### Exports by purpose

| Count | Export family | What the names indicate |
|---:|---|---|
| 5,820 | `log4cxx` decorated C++ API | Loggers, appenders, filters, layouts, rolling policies, XML/configuration, threading, sockets, and ODBC logging. This dominates the count. |
| 172 | `std::` template/runtime exports | Mainly `vector` and allocator instantiations for byte and wide-string containers. |
| 112 | `libssh2_*` C API | SSH sessions, channels, authentication, known-hosts, SCP, and SFTP. |
| 2 | Nexon named C exports | `GetDLLInterface` and `CreateNXAuth`, the SDK interface and authentication factories. |
| **6,106** | **Total** | |

| **6,106** | **Total** | |

### Key mechanisms and VAs

Addresses use the PE image base above; the IDA output is pseudocode, not original source.

- `GetDLLInterface` at `0x18001B670` allocates a `0x390`-byte context, initializes it through `sub_180019750`, stores the context in global `qword_180428DC8`, stores `sub_18001B4A0` in `qword_180428DB8`, and returns the context pointer.
- `sub_180019750` at `0x180019750` sets the `CNXAPIContext` vftable and initializes nested `CNXMessageLooper` and `CNXEventManager` objects plus shared state. Strings also name `CNXAPIProviderBase`, `NXAPIProvider`, `INXAPIProvider`, `CNXAPI`, `INXAPI`, `CNXUser`, `CNXFriend`, `CNXPatcherProgress`, and callback interfaces.
- The shim invokes the returned interface through vtable offsets. It selects a named API using byte offset `0x78` (120); optional initialization uses offset `0x110` (272). This is the dispatch seam between the small shim and this SDK; method names for those raw slots are not recovered.
- `sub_18001B4A0` at `0x18001B4A0` is stored as a callback. It searches for a window with class `_mantis_msg_`, sends message `0x4A` (WM_COPYDATA), posts `0x800B`, then calls context vtable offset `0x80`. This shows a Windows message-based notification/cleanup path; the private message's consumer-side meaning is unknown.
- `CreateNXAuth` at `0x18001E2C0` allocates a `0x38`-byte object and installs the `NXAuth` vftable. The factory's later authentication behavior is not established here.

### Notable imports

The inventory lists 265 imports across eight modules. Most relevant:

| Module | Count | Notable imports / evidence |
|---|---:|---|
| `KERNEL32.dll` | 173 | `LoadLibraryW`, `LoadLibraryA`, `GetProcAddress`, `FreeLibrary`; threads, IO completion ports, processes, files, and synchronization. |
| `ADVAPI32.dll` | 36 | Registry access, token/SID/ACL security, CryptoAPI hashing, and Windows event logging. |
| `WS2_32.dll` | 35 | Winsock sockets, DNS resolution, connect/listen/send/receive. |
| `USER32.dll` | 9 | Window lookup and message send/post, consistent with the `_mantis_msg_` path. |
| `ODBC32.dll` | 7 | All imports are by ordinal in the inventory, so their exact function names are unresolved. |
| `PSAPI.DLL` | 1 | `GetModuleFileNameExW`. |
| `dbghelp.dll` | 1 | `MiniDumpWriteDump`. |
| `VERSION.dll` | 3 | Version-resource query functions. |

The DLL has no imported libcurl, OpenSSL, or libssh2 module; strings and exports instead indicate bundled code, including libcurl 7.30.0 text, OpenSSL material, and the libssh2 API.

### Embedded URLs and product strings

These are static strings, not proof that a request occurs in this build or session.

- API base and identity/auth routes: `http://api.nexon.net`, `/users`, `/profilename`, `/login`, `/auth/ticket`, `/auth/token`, and `http://api.nexon.net/me?access_token=%s`.
- Chat/friends routes: `/chat/friendslist`, `/chat/nickname`, `/chat/message`; local endpoint `http://127.0.0.1:5311`, with `/subscribe/sdk-events`, `/messages/sdk`, `/messages/sdk-events`, and `/chat/friends` strings.
- Commerce/UI: `http://games.nexon.net/ingame_frame.html`, `https://accounts.nexon.net/shop/cart`, `http://jupiter.nexon.net/shop/cart`, and `/nx/purchase-nx/`.
- Product/account fields include `getProductTicket`, `getClientToken`, `productID`, `productActive`, `product_no`, `user_no`, `access_token`, and `refresh_token`; strings also name `NXAPIProvider::GetProductTicket`, `NXAPIProvider::GetProductToken`, and `CNXAPIContext::ManageFriends`.
- Device identity strings include `SOFTWARE\Microsoft\Cryptography`, `MachineGuid`, `THIS-IS-DEFAULT-DEVICE-ID`, and `wmic csproduct get name,identifyingnumber,uuid`. These identify candidate inputs, not which source is actually used.
- Other diagnostic strings include `error during init nxguard on nxsdk` and `nexon_x64.dll init.`

### RE notes

- The 6,106 exports do not imply 6,106 Nexon gameplay APIs. Most are the exported C++ surface of the bundled logging dependency and its template/runtime support; the stable Nexon boundary visible here is `GetDLLInterface` plus `CreateNXAuth`.
- The shim's named interface methods (`get_api`, `get_user`, `get_friends`, `get_patcher`, callbacks, and product/device IDs) are virtual calls into the context selected from this DLL. Follow the shim dossier for the mapping.
- String hits and import tables are static evidence. No network traffic, live module load, registry state, or authentication result was observed.
- Evidence sources: supplied PE inventory, import/export tables, per-DLL string corpus, and IDA pseudocode/xrefs. Embedded PDB basename: `nexon_x64.pdb`.
